import {
	BoxGeometry,
	Vector3
} from 'three';

const _tempNormal = new Vector3();

function getUv( faceDirVector, normal, uvAxis, projectionAxis, radius, sideLength ) {

	const totArcLength = 2 * Math.PI * radius / 4;

	// length of the planes between the arcs on each axis
	const centerLength = Math.max( sideLength - 2 * radius, 0 );
	const halfArc = Math.PI / 4;

	// Get the vector projected onto the Y plane
	_tempNormal.copy( normal );
	_tempNormal[ projectionAxis ] = 0;
	_tempNormal.normalize();

	// total amount of UV space alloted to a single arc
	const arcUvRatio = 0.5 * totArcLength / ( totArcLength + centerLength );

	// the distance along one arc the point is at
	const arcAngleRatio = 1.0 - ( _tempNormal.angleTo( faceDirVector ) / halfArc );

	if ( Math.sign( _tempNormal[ uvAxis ] ) === 1 ) {

		return arcAngleRatio * arcUvRatio;

	} else {

		// total amount of UV space alloted to the plane between the arcs
		const lenUv = centerLength / ( totArcLength + centerLength );
		return lenUv + arcUvRatio + arcUvRatio * ( 1.0 - arcAngleRatio );

	}

}

 "path": "/work/src/rng.js",
 "content": "// Deterministic randomness: every procedural element pulls from a named stream so\n// that two loads of the page produce the same room.\n\nexport class Rng {\n  constructor(seed = 1) {\n    this.s = seed >>> 0 || 1;\n  }\n  // mulberry32\n  next() {\n    this.s = (this.s + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;\n    let t = this.s;\n    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);\n    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);\n    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;\n  }\n  range(a, b) {\n    return a + (b - a) * this.next();\n  }\n  int(a, b) {\n    return Math.floor(this.range(a, b + 1 - 1e-9));\n  }\n  pick(list) {\n    return list[Math.floor(this.next() * list.length) % list.length];\n  }\n  chance(p) {\n    return this.next() < p;\n  }\n  sign() {\n    return this.next() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;\n  }\n  gauss(mean = 0, sd = 1) {\n    const u = Math.max(this.next(), 1e-9);\n    const v = this.next();\n    return mean + sd * Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * v);\n  }\n}\n\n// One fixed seed for the whole scene; streams are derived from it.\nconst SEED = 0xc0ffee;\n\nfunction mix(a, b) {\n  let h = (a ^ 0x9e3779b9) >>> 0;\n  h = Math.imul(h ^ b, 0x85ebca6b) >>> 0;\n  h = (h ^ (h >>> 13)) >>> 0;\n  h = Math.imul(h, 0xc2b2ae35) >>> 0;\n  return (h ^ (h >>> 16)) >>> 0;\n}\n\nexport function stream(name) {\n  let acc = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < name.length; i++) acc = (acc * 31 + name.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;\n  return new Rng(mix(SEED, acc));\n}\n"
}

 "content": "// Everything here paints its own textures on a <canvas>: no image files, no\n// network.  All randomness comes from seeded streams so two loads match.\n\nimport * as THREE from 'three';\nimport { stream } from './rng.js';\n\nfunction canvas(w, h = w) {\n  const c = document.createElement('canvas');\n  c.width = w;\n  c.height = h;\n  return c;\n}\n\nexport function makeTexture(canvasEl, { repeat = [1, 1], srgb = true, aniso = 8, wrap = THREE.RepeatWrapping } = {}) {\n  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvasEl);\n  t.wrapS = t.wrapT = wrap;\n  t.repeat.set(repeat[0], repeat[1]);\n  t.colorSpace = srgb ? THREE.SRGBColorSpace : THREE.NoColorSpace;\n  t.anisotropy = aniso;\n  return t;\n}\n\n// --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// tileable fractal value noise -> Float32Array(w*h) in 0..1\n// --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nexport function fbm(w, h, cells, octaves, seed) {\n  const rng = stream(`noise${seed}-${w}`);\n  const out = new Float32Array(w * h);\n  let amp = 1;\n  let total = 0;\n  for (let o = 0; o < octaves; o++) {\n    const n = Math.max(1, cells << o);\n    const grid = new Float32Array(n * n);\n    for (let i = 0; i < grid.length; i++) grid[i] = rng.next();\n    for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {\n      const fy = (y / h) * n;\n      const iy = Math.floor(fy);\n      const ty = fy - iy;\n      const y0 = ((iy % n) + n) % n;\n      const y1 = (y0 + 1) % n;\n      const sy = ty * ty * (3 - 2 * ty);\n      const row0 = y0 * n;\n      const row1 = y1 * n;\n      const orow = y * w;\n      for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {\n        const fx = (x / w) * n;\n        const ix = Math.floor(fx);\n        const tx = fx - ix;\n        const x0 = ((ix % n) + n) % n;\n        const x1 = (x0 + 1) % n;\n        const sx = tx * tx * (3 - 2 * tx);\n        const a = grid[row0 + x0];\n        const b = grid[row0 + x1];\n        const c = grid[row1 + x0];\n        const d = grid[row1 + x1];\n        const i1 = a + (b - a) * sx;\n        const i2 = c + (d - c) * sx;\n        out[orow + x] += amp * (i1 + (i2 - i1) * sy);\n      }\n    }\n    total += amp;\n    amp *= 0.5;\n  }\n  for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) out[i] /= total;\n  return out;\n}\n\n// bilinear wrap-sample of an fbm field at uv (any range, wraps)\nfunction sample(f, w, h, u, v) {\n  const fx = u * w;\n  const fy = v * h;\n  const ix = Math.floor(fx);\n  const iy = Math.floor(fy);\n  const tx = fx - ix;\n  const ty = fy - iy;\n  const x0 = ((ix % w) + w) % w;\n  const y0 = ((iy % h) + h) % h;\n  const x1 = (x0 + 1) % w;\n  const y1 = (y0 + 1) % h;\n  const a = f[y0 * w + x0];\n  const b = f[y0 * w + x1];\n  const c = f[y1 * w + x0];\n  const d = f[y1 * w + x1];\n  const i1 = a + (b - a) * tx;\n  const i2 = c + (d - c) * tx;\n  return i1 + (i2 - i1) * ty;\n}\n\nconst clamp01 = (x) => (x < 0 ? 0 : x > 1 ? 1 : x);\n\nfunction hsl(h, s, l, out) {\n  const c = new THREE.Color();\n  c.setHSL(h, s, l, THREE.SRGBColorSpace);\n  out.r = c.r;\n  out.g = c.g;\n  out.b = c.b;\n}\n\n// --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// timber: floor boards, shelves, tables.  Planks run along +u.\n// --------------------------------------------------------------------------\nexport function woodMaps({\n  size = 512,\n  planks = 6,\n  rows = 3, // plank segments per row across the tile\n  hue = 0.075,\n  sat = 0.4,\n  light = 0.34,\n  grain = 0.13,\n  roughBase = 0.62,\n  seed = 'wood',\n} = {}) {\n  const rng = stream(seed);\n  const streak = fbm(size, size, 6, 4, `${seed}s`);\n  const fine = fbm(size, size, 60, 3, `${seed}f`);\n  const blotch = fbm(size, size, 3, 2, `${seed}b`);\n\n  // per-row plank phase + per-plank colour jitter\n  const rowPhase = [];\n  const jitter = [];\n  for (let r = 0; r < planks; r++) {\n    rowPhase.push(rng.next() / rows);\n    const j = [];\n    for (let k = 0; k < rows + 1; k++) j.push(rng.next() - 0.5);\n    jitter.push(j);\n  }

 "content": "// Small geometry helpers shared by the room and the furniture.\n\nimport * as THREE from 'three';\nimport { RoundedBoxGeometry } from 'three/addons/geometries/RoundedBoxGeometry.js';\n\nconst _v = new THREE.Vector3();\n\n/** Axis-aligned box built from min/max corners. */\nexport function boxFromBounds(mat, x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, { parent = null } = {}) {\n  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d), mat);\n  mesh.position.set(x, y, z);\n  mesh.castShadow = cast;\n  mesh.receiveShadow = receive;\n  if (parent)  "content": "// A fire: coal bed, crossed logs, shader flames, embers and the flickering\n// light that goes with them.\n\nimport * as THREE from 'three';\nimport { L } from './layout.js';\nimport { box, cyl, sphere } from './geo.js';\nimport { stream } from './rng.js';\n\nconst VERT = /* glsl */ `\n  varying vec2 vUv;\n  void main() {\n    vUv = uv;\n    gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);\n  }\n`;\n\nconst FRAG = /* glsl */ `\n  precision mediump float;\n  varying vec2 vUv;\n  uniform float uTime;\n  uniform float uSeed;\n  uniform float uStrength;\n\n  float hash(vec2 p) {\n    return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);\n  }\n  float noise(vec2 p) {\n    vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p);\n    f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);\n    float a = hash(i), b = hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0));\n    float c = hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), d = hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0));\n    return mix(mix(a, b, f.x), mix(c, d, f.x), f.y);\n  }\n  float fbm(vec2 p) {\n    float s = 0.0, a = 0.5;\n    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { s += a * noise(p); p *= 2.04; a *= 0.5; }\n    return s;\n  }\n\n  void main() {\n    vec2 uv = vUv;\n    float h = uv.y;\n    float n = fbm(vec2(uv.x * 3.4 + uSeed, uv.y * 2.2 - uTime * 1.55));\n    float n2 = fbm(vec2(uv.x * 7.0 - uSeed, uv.y * 4.0 - uTime * 2.6));\n    float dx = abs(uv.x - 0.5) * (1.0 + h * 1.7);\n    float body = (1.0 - h) - dx * 1.45;\n    body += (n - 0.5) * (0.3 + 0.75 * h) + (n2 - 0.5) * 0.18 * h;\n    float a = smoothstep(0.02, 0.4, body);\n    a *= smoothstep(1.0, 0.42, h);          // melt away at the top\n    a *= smoothstep(0.0, 0.10, h);          // and at the very base\n    a *= uStrength;\n    vec3 col = mix(vec3(1.00, 0.52, 0.10), vec3(1.00, 0.86, 0.42), clamp(body * 1.6, 0.0, 1.0));\n    col = mix(vec3(0.55, 0.12, 0.02), col, smoothstep(0.0, 0.30, body));\n    col = mix(col, vec3(1.0, 0.98, 0.88), pow(clamp(body - 0.45, 0.0, 1.0), 2.0));\n    gl_FragColor = vec4(col * a * 1.35, a);\n  }\n`;\n\nexport function buildFire(m) {\n  const rng = stream('fire');\n  const f = L.fire;\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  g.name = 'fire';\n  const bedY = f.opening.y0 + 0.02;\n  const cx = (f.faceX + f.cavityBackX) / 2 + 0.04;\n\n  // coal bed\n  const bed = box(m.ash, f.cavityBackX - 0.06 - (f.faceX + 0.04), 0.03, 0.86, (f.faceX + 0.04 + f.cavityBackX - 0.06) / 2, bedY, 0, {\n    cast: false,\n    receive: true,\n  });\n  g.add(bed);\n  for (let i = 0; // scene.mjs MODE DIR [OUT]
//   preview DIR      the agent's check: render DIR, print a summary, save
//                    DIR/.preview/screenshot.png
//   check DIR OUT    the judged render: write OUT/checks.json,
//                    OUT/screenshot.png and OUT/shots/*.png. Reads optional
//                    OUT/input/check.json {modes: [...], mode_param: "tod"}.
// Both serve DIR on loopback with the site's scene policy (CSP, no outside
// requests) and render in Chromium with software WebGL (SwiftShader).
//
// check (version 2) records, per load: console/page errors, outside
// requests, missing files, frame rate, WebGL draw calls and triangles per
// frame, a blank test; plus a second screenshot 3 s later (animation), a
// second fresh load (determinism), one load per mode via ?<mode_param>=,
// and a click on each mode's button (does the scene change?).
import http from 'node:http';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { chromium } from 'playwright';
import { PNG } from 'pngjs';

const SETTLE_MS = 5000;
const [mode, dirArg, outArg] = process.argv.slice(2);
if (!['preview', 'check'].includes(mode) || !dirArg || (mode === 'check' && !outArg)) {
  console.error('usage: scene.mjs preview DIR | scene.mjs check DIR OUT');
  process.exit(2);
}
const root = path.resolve(dirArg);
const TYPES = {
  '.html': 'text/html', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.mjs': 'text/javascript',
  '.json': 'application/json', '.css': 'text/css', '.png': 'image/png',
  '.jpg': 'image/jpeg', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.glsl': 'text/plain',
  '.vert': 'text/plain', '.frag': 'text/plain', '.wasm': 'application/wasm',
};
// The policy the public site serves scenes with: same-origin files only.
const CSP = [
  "default-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval' blob:",
  "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
  "img-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "connect-src 'self' data: blob:",
  "form-action 'none'",
  "frame-src 'none'",
].join('; ');

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://x');
  let file = path.join(root, decodeURIComponent(url.pathname));
  if (!file.startsWith(root)) { res.writeHead(403).end(); return; }
  if (fs.existsSync(file) && fs.statSync(file).isDirectory()) file = path.join(file, 'index.html');
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) { res.writeHead(404).end(); return; }
  res.writeHead(200, {
    'Content-Type': TYPES[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] || 'application/octet-stream',
    'Content-Security-Policy': CSP,
  });
  fs.createReadStream(file).pipe(res);
});
await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
const origin = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
const indexExists = fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'index.html'));

// Counts draw calls and primitives per animation frame, page-side.
const GL_PROBE = () => {
  const stats = { frames: 0, draws: 0, prims: 0 };
  window.__wcGL = stats;
  const modeVerts = (m, n) => (m === 4 ? n / 3 : m === 5 || m === 6 ? Math.max(0, n - 2) : m === 1 ? n / 2 : n);
  for (const C of [window.WebGLRenderingContext, window.WebGL2RenderingContext]) {
    if (!C) continue;
    const p = C.prototype;
    for (const [fn, countArg, instArg] of [['drawArrays', 2, -1], ['drawElements', 1, -1], ['drawArraysInstanced', 2, 3], ['drawElementsInstanced', 1, 4]]) {
      const orig = p[fn];
      if (!orig) continue;
      p[fn] = function (...a) {
        stats.draws += 1;
        stats.prims += modeVerts(a[0], a[countArg]) * (instArg >= 0 ? a[instArg] : 1);
        return orig.apply(this, a);
      };
    }
  }
  const raf = window.requestAnimationFrame.bind(window);

function analyse(png) {
  const img = PNG.sync.read(png);
  let n = 0, sum = 0, sum2 = 0; const colours = new Set();
  for (let i = 0; i < img.data.length; i += 4 * 7) {
    const [r, g, b] = [img.data[i], img.data[i + 1], img.data[i + 2]];
    const y = 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
    n += 1; sum += y; sum2 += y * y;
    colours.add(((r >> 4) << 8) | ((g >> 4) << 4) | (b >> 4));
  }
  const luma_stddev = Math.round(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, sum2 / n - (sum / n) ** 2)) * 100) / 100;
  return { luma_stddev, distinct_colours: colours.size, blank: luma_stddev < 2 || colours.size < 4 };
}

// Fraction of sampled pixels that differ noticeably between two shots.
function diff(a, b) {
  const A = PNG.sync.read(a), B = PNG.sync.read(b);
  if (A.width !== B.width || A.height !== B.height) return 1;
  let n = 0, d = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < A.data.length; i += 4 * 5) {
    n += 1;
    if (Math.abs(A.data[i] - B.data[i]) > 16 || Math.abs(A.data[i + 1] - B.data[i + 1]) > 16 || Math.abs(A.data[i + 2] - B.data[i + 2]) > 16) d += 1;
  }
  return Math.round((d / n) * 10000) / 10000;
}

const browser = await chromium.launch({
  args: ['--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'],
});

// One fresh page load; returns its record and the screenshot.
async function load(query = '', { measure = true } = {}) {
  const r = { query, console_errors: [], console_warnings: [], page_errors: [], outside_requests: [], missing_files: [] };
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } });
  await page.addInitScript(GL_PROBE);
  await page.route('**/*', (route) => {
    const u = route.request().url();
    if (u.startsWith(origin) || u.startsWith('data:') || u.startsWith('blob:')) return route.continue();
    r.outside_requests.push(u);
    return route.abort();
  });
  page.on('console', (m) => {
    if (m.type() === 'error') r.console_errors.push(m.text().slice(0, 500));
    if (m.type() === 'warning') r.console_warnings.push(m.text().slice(0, 300));
  });
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => r.page_errors.push(String(e).slice(0, 500)));
  page.on('response', (res) => { if (res.status() === 404) r.missing_files.push(res.url().replace(origin, '')); });
  if (indexExists) {
    await page.goto(`${origin}/index.html${query}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 60000 })
      .catch((e) => r.page_errors.push(`load: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 500)));
    await page.waitForTimeout(SETTLE_MS);
    if (measure) {
      Object.assign(r, await page.evaluate(() => new Promise((resolve) => {
        const s0 = { ...(window.__wcGL || {}) }; const t0 = performance.now();
        const done = () => {
          const s = window.__wcGL || {}; const secs = (performance.now() - t0) / 1000;
          const frames = (s.frames || 0) - (s0.frames || 0);
          resolve({
            fps: Math.round(frames / secs * 10) / 10,
            draw_calls_per_frame: frames ? Math.round(((s.draws || 0) - (s0.draws || 0)) / frames) : null,
            primitives_per_frame: frames ? Math.round(((s.prims || 0) - (s0.prims || 0)) / frames) : null,
            canvases: document.querySelectorAll('canvas').length,
          });
        };
        setTimeout(done, 2000);
      })).catch(() => ({ fps: null })));
    }
  }
  const png = await page.screenshot();
  Object.assign(r, analyse(png));
  return { r, png, page };
}

import {
	Color
} from 'three';
import { Pass } from './Pass.js';

/**
 * This class represents a render pass. It takes a camera and a scene and produces
 * a beauty pass for subsequent post processing effects.
 *
 * ```js
 * const renderPass = new RenderPass( scene, camera );
 * composer.addPass( renderPass );
 * ```
 *
 * @augments Pass
 * @three_import import { RenderPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/RenderPass.js';
 */
class RenderPass extends Pass {

	/**
	 * Constructs a new render pass.
	 *
	 * @param {Scene} scene - The scene to render.
	 * @param {Camera} camera - The camera.
	 * @param {?Material} [overrideMaterial=null] - The override material. If set, this material is used
	 * for all objects in the scene.
	 * @param {?(number|Color|string)} [clearColor=null] - The clear color of the render pass.
	 * @param {?number} [clearAlpha=null] - The clear alpha of the render pass.
	 */
	constructor( scene, camera, overrideMaterial = null, clearColor = null, clearAlpha = null ) {

		super();

		/**
		 * The scene to render.
		 *
		 * @type {Scene}
		 */
		this.scene = scene;

		/**
		 * The camera.
		 *
		 * @type {Camera}
		 */
		this.camera = camera;

		/**
		 * The override material. If set, this material is used
		 * for all objects in the scene.
		 *
		 * @type {?Material}
		 * @default null
		 */
		this.overrideMaterial = overrideMaterial;

		/**
		 * The clear color of the render pass.
		 *
		 * @type {?(number|Color|string)}
		 * @default null
		 */
		this.clearColor = clearColor;

		/**
		 * The clear alpha of the render pass.
		 *
		 * @type {?number}
		 * @default null
		 */
		this.clearAlpha = clearAlpha;

		/**
		 * Overwritten to perform a clear operation by default.
		 *
		 * @type {boolean}
		 * @default true
		 */
		this.clear = true;

		/**
		 * If set to `true`, only the depth can be cleared when `clear` is to `false`.
		 *
		 * @type {boolean}
		 * @default false
		 */
		this.clearDepth = false;

		/**
		 * Overwritten to disable the swap.
		 *
		 * @type {boolean}
		 * @default false
		 */
		this.needsSwap = false;

		/**
		 * This flag indicates that this pass renders the scene itself.
		 *
		 * @type {boolean}
		 * @readonly
		 * @default true
		 */
		this.isRenderPass = true;

		this._oldClearColor = new Color();

	}

	/**
	 * Performs a beauty pass with the configured scene and camera.
	 *
	 * @param {WebGLRenderer} renderer - The renderer.
	 * @param {WebGLRenderTarget} writeBuffer - The write buffer. This buffer is intended as the rendering
	 * destination for the pass.
	 * @param {WebGLRenderTarget} readBuffer - The read buffer. The pass can access the result from the
	 * previous pass from this buffer.
	 * @param {number} deltaTime - The delta time in seconds.
	 * @param {boolean} maskActive - Whether masking is active or not.
	 */
	render( renderer, writeBuffer, readBuffer /*, deltaTime, maskActive */ ) {

		const oldAutoClear = renderer.autoClear;
		renderer.autoClear = false;

		let oldClearAlpha, oldOverrideMaterial;

		if ( this.overrideMaterial !== null ) {

			oldOverrideMaterial = this.scene.overrideMaterial;

			this.scene.overrideMaterial = this.overrideMaterial;

		}

		if ( this.clearColor !== null ) {

			renderer.getClearColor( this._oldClearColor );
			renderer.setClearColor( this.clearColor, renderer.getClearAlpha() );

 "newString": "  // a blanket draped over the south arm\n  const armZ = s.halfZ - 0.09;\n  const thr = new THREE.Mesh(\n    drapeGeometry(0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.46, { ripples: 6, amp: 0.016 }),\n    m.throwCloth\n  );\n  thr.position.set(1.0, 0.63, armZ - 0.1);\n  thr.rotation.y = 0.03;\n  thr.castShadow = true;\n  thr.receiveShadow = true;\n  g.add(thr);\n  return g;\n}\n\n/**\n * A piece of cloth hanging over a horizontal edge: the cross-section runs down\n * `inner`, across `top` and down `outer`, with soft folds along `width`.\n */\nfunction drapeGeometry(width, inner, top, outer, { ripples = 5, amp = 0.014, segsU = 14, segsV = 18 } = {}) {\n  const total = inner + top + outer;\n  const verts = [];\n  const idx = [];\n  for (let j = 0; j <= segsV; j++) {\n    const t = (j / segsV) * total;\n    let z;\n    let y;\n    let pz;\n    let py;\n    if (t <= inner) {\n      z = 0;\n      y = -inner + t;\n      pz = -1;\n      py = 0;\n    } else if (t <= inner + top) {\n      z = t - inner;\n      y = 0;\n      pz = 0;\n      py = 1;\n    } else {\n      z = top;\n      y = -(t - inner - top);\n      pz = 1;\n      py = 0;\n    }\n    const droop = Math.min(1, Math.min(t, total - t) / 0.12);\n    for (let i = 0; i <= segsU; i++) {\n      const u = i / segsU;\n      const fold = Math.sin(u * Math.PI * ripples) * amp * droop;\n      verts.push((u - 0.5) * width, y + py * fold, z + pz * fold);\n    }\n  }\n  const row = segsU + 1;\n  for (let j = 0; j < segsV; j++) {\n    for (let i = 0; i < segsU; i++) {\n      const a = j * row + i;\n      idx.push(a, a + 1, a + row, a + 1, a + row + 1, a + row);\n    }\n  }\n  const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n  g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(verts, 3));\n  g.setIndex(idx);\n  g.computeVertexNormals();\n  return g;\n}",

 "newString": "/**\n * Gently rippled cloth panel in the x/y plane, rippling along z.  With\n * `gather` the skirt is pinched into a narrower, deeper-folded column below\n * `gatherAt` (as if tied back).\n */\nexport function curtainGeometry(width, height, { waves = 3.5, amp = 0.055, segsX = 16, segsY = 10, gather = 0, gatherAt = 0.36 } = {}) {\n  const g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width, height, segsX, segsY);\n  const p = g.attributes.position;\n  for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) {\n    const u = p.getX(i);\n    const v = p.getY(i);\n    const t = (height / 2 - v) / height;\n    const k = gather > 0 ? THREE.MathUtils.smoothstep(t, gatherAt, gatherAt + 0.16) * gather : 0;\n    const taper = 0.55 + 0.45 * (0.5 + v / height);\n    p.setX(i, u * (1 - k));\n    p.setZ(i, Math.sin((u / width + 0.5) * Math.PI * 2 * waves) * amp * taper * (1 + 2.4 * k));\n  }\n  g.computeVertexNormals();\n  return g;\n}",

let active = perspRig;

function fitOrtho() {
  const aspect = Math.max(0.2, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight);
  const needW = (L.east - L.west) + 0.5;
  const needH = (L.south - L.north) + 0.5;
  let h = needH;
  let w = h * aspect;
  if (w < needW) {
    w = needW;
    h = w / aspect;
  }
  ortho.left = -w / 2;
  ortho.right = w / 2;
  ortho.top = h / 2;
  ortho.bottom = -h / 2;
  ortho.updateProjectionMatrix();
}

function applyView(name, { announce = true } = {}) {
  const v = VIEWS[name] || VIEWS.door;
  viewName = VIEWS[name] ? name : 'door';
  const top = v.kind === 'ortho';

  if (top) {
    ortho.position.set(0, 9, 0);
    ortho.up.set(0, 0, -1);
    ortho.zoom = 1;
    orthoRig.controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
    fitOrtho();
    ortho.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
    active = orthoRig;
  } else {
    persp.fov = v.fov;
    persp.position.set(...v.pos);
    persp.lookAt(...v.target);
    perspRig.controls.target.set(...v.target);
    persp.updateProjectionMatrix();
    active = perspRig;
  }
  perspRig.controls.enabled = !top;
  orthoRig.controls.enabled = top;
  perspRig.controls.update();
  orthoRig.controls.update();

  room.ceiling.visible = !top;
  room.exterior.visible = !top;
  room.dust.points.visible = !top;
  furniture.pendant.visible = !top;
  bloom.enabled = !top;
  lights.setTopView(top);
  scene.environmentIntensity = top ? 0.5 : 0.16;

  try {
    history.replaceState(null, '', top ? '?view=top' : viewName === 'door' ? '?view=door' : `?view=${viewName}`);
  } catch (e) {
    /* file:// without history access, no problem */
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.view === viewName));
  const cap = document.getElementById('caption');
  if (cap) cap.textContent = v.label;
  const compass = document.getElementById('compass');
  if (compass) compass.classList.toggle('show', top);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// post processing
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const composer = new EffectComposer(renderer);
composer.addPass(new RenderPass(scene, persp));
const bloom = new UnrealBloomPass(new THREE.Vector2(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight), 0.26, 0.6, 0.95);
composer.addPass(bloom);
composer.addPass(new OutputPass());

function syncRenderPass() {
  composer.passes[0].camera = active.cam;
}

function makeDust(m) {
  const rng = stream('dust');
  const N = 320;
  const pos = new Float32Array(N * 3);
  const phase = new Float32Array(N);
  const speed = new Float32Array(N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    pos[i * 3] = rng.range(L.west + 0.3, L.east - 0.2);
    pos[i * 3 + 1] = rng.range(0.25, L.height - 0.2);
    pos[i * 3 + 2] = rng.range(L.north + 0.3, L.south - 0.3);
    phase[i] = rng.next() * Math.PI * 2;
    speed[i] = rng.range(0.006, 0.028);
  }
  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));

a0, thick, u1 - u0);\n  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);\n  if (axis === 'x') mesh.position.set((a0 + a1) / 2, y - thick / 2, (u0 + u1) / 2);\n  else mesh.position.set(y - thick / 2, (u0 + u1) / 2, (a0 + a1) / 2);\n  mesh.receiveShadow = true;\n  parent.add(mesh);\n  return mesh;\n}\n\nfunction floorBoards(m) {\n  const g = new THREE.Group();\n  g.name = 'floor-boards';\n  const gap = 0.008;\n  const bw = 0.19;\n  let z = NZ;\n  let n = 0;\n  while (z < SZ - q);\n        if (b0 > zz[0] && b0 < zz[1]) {\n          segs.push([zz[0], b0]);\n          segs.push([b0, zz[1]]);\n        } else segs.push([zz[0], zz[1]]);\n      } else segs.push([z0, z1]);\n    } else {\n      segs.push([z0, z1]);\n    }\n    for (const [s0, s1] of segs) {\n      if (Math.abs(s1 - 1: // Small geometry helpers shared by the room and the furniture.
2: 
3: import * as THREE from 'three';
4: import { RoundedBoxGeometry } from 'three/addons/geometries/RoundedBoxGeometry.js';
5: 
6: const _v = new THREE.Vector3();
7: 
8: /** Cheap guard: a NaN in a transform makes geometry vanish or explode. */
9: function chk(x, y, z, tag) {
10:   if (!Number.isFinite(x) || !Number.isFinite(y) || !Number.isFinite(z)) {
11:     throw new Error(`non-finite position for ${tag}: ${x}, ${y}, ${z}`);
12:   }
13: }
14: 
15: /** Axis-aligned box built from min/max corners. */
16: export function boxFromBounds(mat, x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, { cast = false, receive = true, name = '' } = {}) {
17:   chk((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2, name || 'boxFromBounds');
18:   const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(Math.max(1e-4, x1 - x0), Math.max(1e-4, y1 - y0), Math.max(1e-4, z1 - z0));
19:   const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat);
20:   mesh.position.set((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2);
21:   mesh.castShadow = cast;
22:   mesh.receiveShadow = receive;
23:   mesh.name = name;
24:   return mesh;
25: }
26: 
27: export function box(mat, w, h, d, x, y, z, { cast = false, receive = true, parent = null } = {}) {
28:   chk(x, y, z, 'box');
29:   const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(Math.max(1e-4, w), Math.max(1e-4, h), Math.max(1e-4, d)), mat);
30:   mesh.position.set(x, y, z);

37: export function rbox(mat, w, h, d, x, y, z, r = 0.04, seg = 3, { cast = true, receive = true, parent = null } = {}) {
38:   chk(x, y, z, 'rbox');
39:   const rr = Math.min(r, w / 2 - 1e-3, h / 2 - 1e-3, d / 2 - 1e-3);
40:   const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new RoundedBoxGeometry(w, h, d, seg, Math.max(0.002, rr)), mat);
41:   mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
42:   mesh.castShadow = cast;
43:   mesh.receiveShadow = receive;

45:   return mesh;

48: export function cyl(mat, rTop, rBottom, h, x, y, z, { seg = 16, cast = true, receive = true, parent = null, open = false } = {}) {
49:   chk(x, y, z, 'cyl');
50:   const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(rTop, rBottom, h, seg, 1, open), mat);
51:   mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
52:   mesh.castShadow = cast;
53:   mesh.receiveShadow = receive;
54:   if (parent) parent.add(mesh);
55:   return mesh;

58: export function sphere(mat, r, x, y, z, { seg = 18, cast = true, parent = null } = {}) {
59:   chk(x, y, z, 'sphere');
60:   const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(r, seg, Math.max(8, seg / 2)), mat);
61:   mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
62:   mesh.castShadow = cast;
63:   mesh.receiveShadow = true;
64:   if (parent) parent.add(mesh);
65:   return mesh;

68: /** Cylinder lying along an axis ('x' | 'z' | 'y'). */
69: export function rod(mat, axis, len, r, x, y, z, { seg = 12, cast = true, parent = null } = {}) {
70:   chk(x, y, z, 'rod');
71:   const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r, r, len, seg), mat);
72:   mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
73:   if (axis === 'x') mesh.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
74:   if (axis === 'z') mesh.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
75:   mesh.castShadow = cast;
76:   mesh.receiveShadow = true;
77:   if (parent) parent.add(mesh);
78:   return mesh;
79: }
80: 
81: /**
82:  * Gently rippled cloth panel in the x/y plane, rippling along z.  With
83:  * `gather` the skirt is pinched into a narrower, deeper-folded column below
84:  * `gatherAt` (as if tied back).
85:  */
86: export function curtainGeometry(width, height, { waves = 3.5, amp = 0.055, segsX = 16, segsY = 10, gather = 0, gatherAt = 0.36 } = {}) {
87:   const g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width, height, segsX, segsY);
88:   const p = g.attributes.position;
89:   for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) {
90:     const u = p.getX(i);
91:     const v = p.getY(i);
92:     const t = (height / 2 - v) / height;
93:     const k = gather > 0 ? THREE.MathUtils.smoothstep(t, gatherAt, gatherAt + 0.16) * gather : 0;
94:     const taper = 0.55 + 0.45 * (0.5 + v / height);
95:     p.setX(i, u * (1 - k));
96:     p.setZ(i, Math.sin((u / width + 0.5) * Math.PI * 2 * waves) * amp * taper * (1 + 2.4 * k));
97:   }
98:   g.computeVertexNormals();
99:   return g;
100: }
101: 
102: /** Tapered, slightly bent tree branch / stem. */
103: export function stemGeometry(len, r0, r1, bend = 0.2, segs = 6) {
104:   const g = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r1, r0, len, 6, segs, false);
105:   const p = g.attributes.position;
106:   for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) {
107:     const t = (p.getY(i) + len / 2) / len;
108:     p.setX(i, p.getX(i) + bend * t * t);
109:   }
110:   g.computeVertexNormals();
111:   return g;
112: }
113: 
114: export function disposeGroup(group) {
115:   group.traverse((o) => {
116:     if (o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose();
117:   });
118: }

1: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
2: // The plan, as numbers.  North is -z, east is +x, y is up, metres, origin =
3: // centre of the floor.  Every piece of geometry reads its dimensions from here
4: // so the whole scene is one editable spec.
5: //
6: //   x: -3 (west wall) ..................... +3 (east wall)      6.0 m
7: //   z: -2.25 (north wall) ................. +2.25 (south wall)  4.5 m
8: //   y: 0 (floor) ...................... 2.7 (ceiling)
9: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
10: 
11: export const L = {
12:   // ---- shell ------------------------------------------------------------
13:   west: -3,
14:   east: 3,
15:   north: -2.25,
16:   south: 2.25,
17:   height: 2.7,
18:   wallT: 0.12, // walls sit outside the interior faces
19: 
20:   // ---- openings ---------------------------------------------------------
21:   // Door: south wall, 0.9 m wide, centre 1.2 m from the west wall.
22:   // Hinged on its west side and it opens into the room.
23:   door: {

157: export function withinRoom(r, eps = 0.002) {
158:   return (
159:     r.x0 >= L.west - eps &&
160:     r.x1 <= L.east + eps &&
161:     r.z0 >= L.north - eps &&
162:     r.z1 <= L.south + eps &&
163:     r.h <= L.height + eps
164:   );
165: }
166: 
167: export function overlaps(a, b, eps = 0.004) {
168:   return a.x0 < b.x1 - eps && a.x1 > b.x0 + eps && a.z0 < b.z1 - eps && a.z1 > b.z0 + eps;
169: }
170: 
171: // Door swing: quarter disc of radius 0.9 centred on the hinge, swept into the
172: // room from the south wall to the line x = hingeX.
173: export function inDoorSwing(r, pad = 0) {
174:   const cx = L.door.hingeX;
175:   const cz = L.south;
176:   const rad = L.door.x1 - L.door.x0 + pad;
177:   if (r.x1 < cx || r.z1 < cz) return false;
178:   let best = Infinity;
179:   for (const x of [r.x0, r.x1]) {
180:     for (const z of [r.z0, r.z1]) {
181:       if (x >= cx && z <= cz) best = Math.min(best, Math.hypot(x - cx, z - cz));
182:     }
183:   }
184:   return best <= rad;
185: }
186: 
187: // Prints a little table of clearances; only used with ?debug=1.
188: export function validate() {
189:   const problems = [];
190:   for (const f of FOOTPRINTS) {
191:     if (!withinRoom(f)) problems.push(`${f.name}: outside the room`);
192:     if (inDoorSwing(f)) problems.push(`${f.name}: inside the door swing`);
193:   }
194:   for (let i = 0; i < FOOTPRINTS.length; i++) {
195:     for (let j = i + 1; j < FOOTPRINTS.length; j++) {
196:       const a = FOOTPRINTS[i];
197:       const b = FOOTPRINTS[j];
198:       if (overlaps(a, b)) {
199:         const gap = Math.max(a.z0 - b.z1, b.z0 - a.z1, a.x0 - b.x1, b.x0 - a.x1);
200:         problems.push(`${a.name} overlaps ${b.name} (${gap.toFixed(2)} m)`);
201:       }
202:     }
203:   }
204:   const chairNearestZ = -1.072; // the armchair's front-left corner, from layout.

10: const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
11:   const url = new URL(req.url, 'http://x');
12:   let file = path.join(root, decodeURIComponent(url.pathname));
13:   if (!file.startsWith(root)) { res.writeHead(403).end(); return; }
14:   if (fs.existsSync(file) && fs.statSync(file).isDirectory()) file = path.join(file, 'index.html');
15:   if (!fs.existsSync(file)) { res.writeHead(404).end(); return; }
16:   res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': TYPES[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] || 'application/octet-stream' });
17:   fs.createReadStream(file).pipe(res);

output (615 chars)
function buildHud() {
  const bar = document.getElementById('views');
  for (const key of ['door', 'top', 'hearth', 'reading']) {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.dataset.view = key;
    b.textContent = key;
    b.addEventListener('click', () => applyView(key));
    bar.appendChild(b);
  }
  window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (!e.key) return;
    const keys = { 1: 'door', 2: 'top', 3: 'hearth', 4: 'reading', d: 'door', t: 'top', f: 'hearth', r: 'reading' };
    const v = keys[e.key.toLowerCase()];
    if (v) applyView(v);
  });
}

      result.modes.push(one.r);
      await one.page.close();

    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'screenshot.png'), main.png);
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, 'checks.json'), JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));

const browser = await chromium.launch({
  args: ['--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'],

// One fresh page load; returns its record and the screenshot.
async function load(query = '', { measure = true } = {}) {
  const r = { query, console_errors: [], console_warnings: [], page_errors: [], outside_requests: [], missing_files: [] };
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 800 } });
  await page.addInitScript(GL_PROBE);
  await page.route('**/*', (route) => {
    const u = route.request().url();
    if (u.startsWith(origin) || u.startsWith('data:') || u.startsWith('blob:')) return route.continue();
    r.outside_requests.push(u);
    return route.abort();
  });
  page.on('console', (m) => {
    if (m.type() === 'error') r.console_errors.push(m.text().slice(0, 500));
    if (m.type() === 'warning') r.console_warnings.push(m.text().slice(0, 300));
  });
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => r.page_errors.push(String(e).slice(0, 500)));
  page.on('response', (res) => { if (res.status() === 404) r.missing_files.push(res.url().replace(origin, '')); });
  if (indexExists) {
    await page.goto(`${origin}/index.html${query}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 60000 })
      .catch((e) => r.page_errors.push(`load: ${e.message}`.slice(0, 500)));
    await page.waitForTimeout(SETTLE_MS);
    if (measure) {
      Object.assign(r, await page.evaluate(() => new Promise((resolve) => {
        const s0 = { ...(window.__wcGL || {}) }; const t0 = performance.now();
        const done = () => {
          const s = window.__wcGL || {}; const secs = (performance.now() - t0) / 1000;
          const frames = (s.frames || 0) - (s0.frames || 0);
          resolve({
            fps: Math.round(frames / secs * 10) / 10,
            draw_calls_per_frame: frames ? Math.round(((s.draws || 0) - (s0.draws || 0)) / frames) : null,
            primitives_per_frame: frames ? Math.round(((s.prims || 0) - (s0.prims || 0)) / frames) : null,
            canvases: document.querySelectorAll('canvas').length,
          });
        };
        setTimeout(done, 2000);
      })).catch(() => ({ fps: null })));
    }
  }

  function update(t) {
    // a slow swell under the quick flicker: the fire breathes as the logs settle
    const swell = 0.9 + 0.13 * Math.sin(t * 1.15 + 0.9) + 0.05 * Math.sin(t * 0.53);
    const flick =
      swell +
      0.16 * Math.sin(t * 6.9) +
      0.11 * Math.sin(t * 2.9 + 1.3) +
      0.07 * Math.sin(t * 12.7 + 0.4) +
      0.05 * Math.sin(t * 21.3 + 2.1);
    const k = Math.max(0.45, Math.min(1.35, flick));
    light.intensity = 8.5 * k;
    spill.intensity = 2.2 * k;
    dance.intensity = 9 * k;
    // the sweep periods are deliberately not near 3 s, so the light is always
    // somewhere new rather than keep pace with a stopwatch
    dance.target.position.set(
      1.85 + 0.72 * Math.sin(t * 0.9) + 0.38 * Math.sin(t * 2.6 + 1.1),
      0.02,
      0.68 * Math.sin(t * 1.3 + 0.4) + 0.42 * Math.sin(t * 2.2)
    );
    for (const mat of flames) {
      mat.uniforms.uTime.value = t;
      mat.uniforms.uStrength.value = Math.min(1.45, Math.max(0.6, 0.7 + 0.55 * (k - 0.72)));
    }
    glow.material.opacity = 0.45 + 0.25 * (k - 0.6);
    const s = 1.35 + 0.35 * k;
    glow.scale.set(s, s * 1.05, 1);
    // the slow term only: the quick flicker stays at the hearth, while this is
    // what the rest of the room is allowed to breathe with
    return swell;
  }
